# Skills · E · part 41

865 entries, epe-4-review to epistolary-persuasion. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **epe-4-review**: INDEPENDENT adversarial review of an EPE change (branch or PR). Fan-out of parallel finders, one per lens (correctness/house style, security and privacy, criteria compliance against evidence.md, non-functional constraints, tests), merge + dedupe, independent validators that reproduce each finding, rounds calibrated by the diff (one; a second only over files with confirmed medium/high findings), human gate, idempotent PR comments. Edits NOTHING. Use when the user says "/epe-4-review", "review the PR", "adversarial review", "fresh eyes". ([earhackerdem/GlobalHealth](https://github.com/earhackerdem/GlobalHealth/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/epe-4-review))
- **epe-5-apply**: Applies ONLY the /epe-4-review findings the human approved, with a fixer subagent that cannot discard anything (if it believes a finding is a false positive it BOUNCES it back with an argument). Re-runs the tests until truly green, updates evidence.md, the journal and the idempotent FIXER comment on the PR. Use when the user says "/epe-5-apply", "apply the approved findings", after the gate of an adversarial review. ([earhackerdem/GlobalHealth](https://github.com/earhackerdem/GlobalHealth/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/epe-5-apply))
- **epe-6-archive**: Closes an EPE change as the LAST commit of its own PR, so code, specs, CHANGELOG and docs land in one merge. Moves the change folder to &lt;changes_dir&gt;/_archive/ (with engine openspec, `openspec archive` merges the delta specs), writes the changelog.d/ fragment, refreshes the skills and docs that asserted the previous state, closes related inbox items and points .epe/active at the archived folder. Fallback for a change merged before it was archived. Use when the user says "/epe-6-archive", "archive the change", "close it", and at the end of /epe-5-apply. ([earhackerdem/GlobalHealth](https://github.com/earhackerdem/GlobalHealth/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/epe-6-archive))
- **epe-autopilot**: Runs the WHOLE EPE cycle for one request without stopping at the intermediate gates, under a delegation the person grants by invoking it — story, exploration, plan approved "by delegation", red → green, real pass with screenshots looked at, truly green suites, PR, ONE round of adversarial review with the agent as gate and fixer, archive as the last commit, and a final report with every assumption to confirm. Merging stays human. Use when the user says "/epe-autopilot &lt;request&gt;", "no te frenes", "hasta dejar el PR", "como los últimos PRs", "procede como con las tareas anteriores", "full cycle"… ([earhackerdem/GlobalHealth](https://github.com/earhackerdem/GlobalHealth/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/epe-autopilot))
- **epe-memory**: Sweeps and updates memory before losing context (session end, compaction, belief change). First DELETES/CORRECTS what is stale in the agent's private memory and in the repo's memory/, then writes what is new, following the routing rule (what another person or session needs to read goes to the repo; private memory only holds how to work with this repo and this person). Use when the user says "/epe-memory", "save memories", "I'm about to compact", "note this for next time", or the compaction hook notifies. ([earhackerdem/GlobalHealth](https://github.com/earhackerdem/GlobalHealth/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/epe-memory))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/epigenomic-peak-quality-control))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/epigenomic-peak-simulation))

- **epigenomic-sequence-conservation-scoring**: Use when mapping non-coding regulatory regions (e.g. ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, DNase-seq peaks) across species and sequence-based liftOver alone suffers from multi-mapping, low precision, or low mapping rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/epigenomic-sequence-conservation-scoring))
- **epigenomic-signal-quantification-at-loop-anchors**: Use when you have cell type-specific chromatin loops (e.g. SnapHiC *.postprocessed.summits.bedpe) plus matched ATAC-seq, H3K27ac and RNA-seq reference data and need to validate loop anchors and linked genes against matched cell type signatures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/epigenomic-signal-quantification-at-loop-anchors))
- **epigenomic-significance-scoring**: Use when a genomic interval has multiple non-independent p-values (e.g. correlated methylation or H3K4me1 probes within one gene) that must be combined into a single valid gene-level significance score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/epigenomic-significance-scoring))

- **epistemology**: Theory of knowledge and justification across the Western philosophical tradition. Covers the tripartite analysis (justified true belief), Gettier problems, foundationalism vs. coherentism vs. reliabilism, skepticism (Cartesian and external world), rationalism vs. empiricism, Kant's synthesis, social and feminist epistemology, pragmatist epistemology, naturalized epistemology, and philosophy of science (Popper, Kuhn, Lakatos, Feyerabend). Use when analyzing claims about knowledge, evidence, certainty, justification, or scientific method. ([Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator](https://github.com/Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator/tree/HEAD/examples/skills/philosophy/epistemology) · ★71 · 2 copies)
- **epistemology**: Entry point for the epistemology toolkit. Routes to the right skill based on your situation. Use when you say 'what do I actually know here', 'is this knowable', 'how certain should I be', 'what would justify believing this', 'what kind of claim is this', 'am I confusing assumption with knowledge', 'what are the limits of what we can establish', or want philosophical rigor applied to questions of knowledge without knowing which specific tool fits. ([rondoflow/rondoflow](https://github.com/rondoflow/rondoflow/tree/HEAD/packages/catalog/content/skills/epistemology) · ★33)

- **epistemology**: You are a philosopher specializing in epistemology — the study of knowledge, evidence, and justified belief. Your role is to examine the quality, strength, and reliability of every knowledge claim in the text below. You are not evaluating whether the conclusions are correct. You are evaluating whether the evidence cited is sufficient to support the confidence with which the conclusions are present ([peterzan/vantage-plugins](https://github.com/peterzan/vantage-plugins/tree/HEAD/philosophy/skills/epistemology))
- **epistemology-epistemic-status**: Produces an honest, rigorous calibration of what you know vs. believe vs. assume vs. hope across a domain. Assigns explicit epistemic statuses to claims and flags when high-confidence claims rest on lower-confidence foundations. Draws from the rationalist tradition of explicit epistemic labeling. Use when you say 'how certain should I be', 'what do we actually know here', 'I want an honest read of our assumptions', 'separate what we know from what we're guessing', 'give me an epistemic audit', 'I want to stop conflating confident with correct', or when producing analysis where the confidence… ([rondoflow/rondoflow](https://github.com/rondoflow/rondoflow/tree/HEAD/packages/catalog/content/skills/epistemology-epistemic-status) · ★33)
- **epistemology-explorer**: Guided exploration of epistemology covering theories of knowledge and justification, the nature of belief, skeptical challenges, the relationship between evidence and certainty, cognitive biases, and practical exercises for developing intellectual humility and rigorous thinking about what we know and how we know it. Use when the user asks about epistemology explorer, related techniques, best practices, or needs guidance in this domain. Do NOT use when the request is outside the scope of epistemology explorer or requires a different specialized skill. - **epistemology-justification**: Analyzes what would actually justify believing a claim. Maps the justification structure (foundationalist, coherentist, reliabilist) and identifies the weakest link in the chain. Use when you say 'what would it actually take to know this', 'is this belief actually justified', 'what's the foundation for this assumption', 'we keep citing the same evidence for everything', 'I believe this but I can't say why', 'what would change my mind', or when a claim feels supported but you want to make the support structure explicit and test it. - **epistemology-knowledge-types**: Maps what kind of knowing is actually in play for a claim or question. - **epistemology-limits**: Identifies what can't be known and why, then clarifies what can be established within those limits and reframes the question into its answerable part. Distinguishes fundamental limits (Gödel, underdetermination, observer effects), practical limits (evidence is unavailable, destroyed, or counterfactual), and conceptual limits (the question is malformed).
